# Requirements

project: VTT

A requirement is a rule the platform must obey, written once and cited by
whatever proves it. An id is allocated by `requirement-id` and never chosen by
hand; the evidence cell is never blank. How ids are allocated, cited and
checked: `docs/specifications/008-requirement-ids-come-from-the-dispenser.md`.

| Id | Requirement | Verified by |

| VTT-665 | WITHDRAWN 2026-11-07: A Say line whose first word is `/w`, in any case, reads as a whisper to the next word, with the rest of the line, trimmed, as its text; or, when that next word opens with a double quote, to the words between it and the next double quote, trimmed, with the rest of the line after that one, trimmed, as its text, or, when no later double quote closes it, to the rest of the line after it, trimmed, with no text. | VTT-739 | WITHDRAWN 2026-11-15: An actor the world adds again after a removal is introduced afresh to a viewer that sees it when the viewer no longer holds the id, or holds one that differs from what the viewer may be sent of the new actor, and is corrected otherwise. For a party member that is its name, module id, attributes, module data, resource names or maxima, and not its ability ids, which are corrected; for any other actor, its module id. | VTT-794 | WITHDRAWN 2026-11-16: In a sight correction of a party member's resources, a name's `ResourceRemoved` comes before its `ResourceAdded`, a name held at the same max at another current is sent one `ResourceChanged` in its place in the byte order, and each frame carries the event's sequence and no other envelope field. False as worded where the difference does not fit int32, which is sent as two `ResourceChanged` through zero, and where the world's current is below zero or above a max above zero, which no `ResourceChanged` can land on. Succeeded by VTT-796. | **READING — the reviewer of Task 2 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-15-an-ability-is-given-with-its-value.md, 2026-11-16; withdrawn, held by nothing** |
| VTT-795 | WITHDRAWN 2026-11-16: In a sight correction of a party member's resources, a name's `ResourceRemoved` comes before its `ResourceAdded`; a name held at the same max at another current is sent, in its place in the byte order, one `ResourceChanged`, or two through zero when the difference does not fit int32, when the world's current is at least zero and at most a max above zero, and a `ResourceRemoved` and a `ResourceAdded` otherwise; each frame carries the event's sequence and no other envelope field. False as worded for a resource with no max: it has no max above zero to be at most, so the row reads as sending it a removal and an addition, where one `ResourceChanged` is sent. Succeeded by VTT-796. | **READING — the reviewer of Task 2 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-15-an-ability-is-given-with-its-value.md, 2026-11-16; withdrawn, held by nothing** |
